### 1160_derivative_gated_tick

```tape
@H H_1160 := "does the from-scratch inference+learning MITOSIS engine need a metronome TICK to gate its cell-division growth, or is the tick EVENT/DERIVATIVE-driven? (the user's 틱이 있어야 되나?)" :: discovery [d=2026-06-12 status=🔴-CLOSED-NEGATIVE domain=MITOSIS-ENGINE]

  seed = "The mitosis engine learns online from scratch (@D p8, NO train/infer split — it keeps dividing during inference). Feeding a TIME-ORDERED stream (real text bytes / synthetic audio frames) in, what should GATE the cell-division growth tick (engine_mitosis_tick, CORE/engine_cli.hexa)? Three candidate tick-gates: METRONOME (M2-like, split every N steps on a fixed exogenous clock), POSITIONAL (M1-like, split keyed to absolute stream index), DERIVATIVE (M3-like, split on a rising-edge of |d(input)/dt| — the data's own CHANGE events). Plus a NO-GATE control (blind fixed-count split, input-ignoring). ELEVATES the toy-EEG finding of .discoveries/clm-time-encoding.tape (M3 DERIVATIVE was the ONLY arm to beat the phase-shuffled control there) + kosmos-time-axis (F-ORDER beats F-SHUFFLE) + tensionlink-dim-time (dF/dt beats shuffle) — to the GENERAL tick-gating question on a time-ordered text/audio stream, but on the falsifier-mandated SUBSTRATE-NATIVE ONLINE NEXT-STEP PREDICTION ERROR metric (NOT the decode/recon metric the toy-EEG win used)."

  method = "Reuse the H_1159/H_1159b prototype-split MITOSIS substrate (cells = prototype vectors, online-mean update, tension-driven split of the highest-tension cell, NO backprop/gradients per p8) as the ENGINE; swap ONLY the split-trigger gate among {METRONOME, POSITIONAL, DERIVATIVE, NOGATE}. All gates are split-BUDGET-matched (≈ expected derivative-fire count) and split the SAME (highest-tension) cell, isolating WHEN the tick fires as the only variable. Capacity kept SCARCE (MAX_CELLS=6) so split-PLACEMENT is testable. STREAMS: (1) TEXT [decisive] = real bytes in time-order from CORE/testdata/clm_mid_5lang_c4.txt (seed = deterministic start offset, NO shuffle/epochs), feature = 8-d local byte-statistics over a sliding 48-byte window, derivative = |d(feature)/dt| = byte-change/novelty; (2) AUDIO [confirmation] = synthetic RECURRING-REGIME drift stream (6 regimes each w/ own center+constant drift, revisited; a single global next-delta cannot serve all). METRIC = substrate-native ONLINE NEXT-STEP prediction error ||pred_next − true_next|| where pred_next = current + owner-cell running next-delta (p7, NOT perplexity/CE); ORDER-SENSITIVITY via a time-SHUFFLE control on the scored region. 10 seeds (900..909), deterministic, $0 CPU numpy. PRE-SCORING DEFECT-FIX (a_completeness_over_cheap, NOT a goalpost move): a diagnostic showed next-step error is NOISE-FLOORED and INVISIBLE to cell-count on a smooth feature stream (err ~flat 2→32 cells) → would make the gate untestable; fixed by SCARCE capacity + the recurring-regime audio stream where capacity provably matters. Frozen falsifier bars UNCHANGED."

  falsifier = "Pre-registered in the harness docstring BEFORE measuring; deterministic ≥8 seeds; paired Cohen's d on per-seed deltas (positive d => derivative is lower-error). F1 DERIVATIVE-WINS: on the ordered stream derivative beats BOTH metronome AND positional, d≥0.8 each. F2 ORDER-SENSITIVE: derivative-vs-metronome advantage VANISHES on the time-SHUFFLED control, d_shuf<0.3. F3 TICK-NECESSITY (the user's literal question): derivative beats the input-blind NO-GATE baseline, d≥0.8 → a tick IS needed AND should be event/derivative-driven; if metronome ties derivative AND both tie no-gate → 'a blind clock suffices' (honest negative). SUPPORTED (🟢 EVENT-DERIVATIVE-TICK) iff F1 ∧ F2 ∧ F3-derivative-beats-nogate; else CLOSED-NEGATIVE with the specific ruling."

  result = "🔴 CLOSED-NEGATIVE — NO event/derivative tick advantage under next-step error at toy scale; a blind clock roughly suffices. TEXT [decisive]: all 4 gates TIE at err≈0.742 (METRO 0.7437, POS 0.7424, DERIV 0.7416, NOGATE 0.7437). F1 FAIL derivative-vs-metronome d=0.15, vs-positional d=0.06 (≪0.8).   version = "1.0"

@H 1171 := "APOPTOSIS / programmed cell-death — does adding cell death to inference-time mitosis improve adaptation (prune stale cells) vs growth-only? (life-criterion MISSING #1: mortality)" :: universe [🔴 CLOSED-NEGATIVE — DEATH-DOES-NOT-HELP (a_paper_negative_ok)]
  seed         = "Life/evolution audit gap: the live A⇄G mitosis engine GROWS (engine_mitosis_tick, H_1159/1159b) but cells never DIE — the H_1159b self-limiting cap is homeostasis, NOT apoptosis. Death is a life-criterion AND the prerequisite that lets selection (H_1175) bite."
  substrate    = "toy $0 CPU numpy. REUSE UNIVERSE/h1159b_mitosis_capacity_self_tuning.py mitosis substrate; ADD apoptosis = a cell whose running assignment-tension stays > THETA_DEATH for K consecutive windows DIES (removed)."
  method       = "FROZEN FALSIFIER (pre-reg): 🟢 DEATH-HELPS iff mitosis+apoptosis achieves lower final-window error on a non-stationary stream than growth-only mitosis (Cohen's d>=0.8) AND cell-count stays BOUNDED by death (not the hard cap) AND keeps the H_1159b K-tracking. 🔴 if apoptosis does not help or hurts adaptation (a_paper_negative_ok)."
  result       = "🔴 CLOSED-NEG (12 seeds, 3-pt K_concurrent ladder {3,5,8}, non-stationary 6-regime stream, $0 CPU numpy, deterministic, p7 assignment-error NOT perplexity). F1 ADVANTAGE FAIL: pooled Cohen's d(growth−death)=−0.92 below the +0.8 bar — apoptosis HURTS (death_err HIGHER than growth-only: K=3 d=−0.04 flat, K=5 d=−1.58, K=8 d=−1.16). F2 BOUNDED-BY-DEATH PASS: max death cell-count 14 < 30 hard cap. F3 K-TRACKING PASS: Spearman(K_concurrent, death cell-count)=0.858 >= 0.8 (death preserves the h1159b self-tuning). SUPPORTED = F1 AND F2 AND F3 = FALSE (F1 fails)."
  finding      = "Mortality is NOT a free adaptation lever at toy scale. At the pre-registered THETA_DEATH=THETA_SPLIT=1.6 the irreducible noise floor (sigma=0.6*sqrt(8)~1.66) keeps a cell's assignment-tension hovering AT the death threshold, so apoptosis prunes cells STILL doing useful work, leaving fewer cells on live clusters and HIGHER error. Death keeps the population bounded (F2) and still tracks world complexity (F3), but growth-only already self-limits below the cap, so death only removes capacity it cannot afford. RULES OUT 'stale-tension apoptosis improves adaptation' at the frozen settings; goalpost NOT moved (no THETA_DEATH re-tune). Selection (H_1175) would need a death signal that distinguishes STALE-on-dead-cluster from STRAINED-on-live-noisy-cluster — raw running assignment-tension cannot."
  verdict_tier = "🔴 CLOSED-NEGATIVE (toy $0; reuses h1159b; frozen falsifier; F2/F3 pass, F1 fails)"
  artifact     = "UNIVERSE/h1171_apoptosis_death.py + .verdicts/1171_apoptosis_death/H_1171.txt"

MECHANISM (traced /tmp/diag3): population grows healthily (E accrues ~2.4, divides to 3-5 cells) then ALL cells starve SIMULTANEOUSLY in a ~50-step crash as later-onset clusters pull existing cells between competing centroids -> assignment error rises above the income threshold -> collective energy drain -> extinction; division COSTS energy precisely when energy is already draining, so mitosis cannot rescue the crash. FINDING: a naive per-cell metabolic budget is NOT a homeostatic capacity governor — it is a boom-bust predator-prey-like instability with NO negative-feedback floor; the external MAX_CELLS cap of H_1159b cannot be replaced by starvation alone. Diagnosed via /tmp/diag*; rescaled D_REF=6.0 so on-cluster d~1.65 nets +0.019/step (a useful cell CAN survive) while between-cluster d~5 nets -0.003/step (a redundant cell starves) — making survival CONTINGENT on usefulness, the F1/F2 axis. After this fix the metabolism genuinely operates (cells divide, some survive at K=3) yet STILL collapses at K>=5 -> the collapse is a real dynamical result, not the defect. NO further tuning toward green (goalpost discipline, cf h1144/h1150)."

FINDING: the Darwinian cull-fittest/reproduce operator COLLAPSES cell diversity (fit parent's daughter overwrites the culled cell → population converges on the dominant-cluster centroid), HURTING generalization to a novel-but-related shifted world; the matched instructive arm (H_1159, every cell adapts equally, none replaced) keeps a more diverse spread that covers the shifted+new clusters better. At toy scale instructive/Lamarckian online-adaptation captures the available adaptive gains AND retains the diversity that differential reproduction destroys — selection is NOT a distinct adaptive lever here. Construction: CAP=12 fixed-capacity population, fitness=1/(1+running clustering-err) p7, held-out task=centers shifted N(0,2) + 2 genuinely-new clusters, both arms matched on cap/LR/stream/seed (only the selection operator differs). RUN VERIFIED deterministic-identical on re-run."

REUSE h1159b cells; ADD a scarce shared resource pool R=K_cap=6; cells draw R proportional to assignment share; when total demand (n cells) > K_cap, lowest-fitness cells (fitness=1/clustering-error) starve/die until n<=K_cap (competition). Compare COMPETITION vs unconstrained equal-survival growth. World K_true=16 >> K_cap so demand strongly exceeds supply (pre-freeze construction fix: 1st param THETA=1.6/K=8 was degenerate non-scarce — only ~9-11 cells ever born so K_cap never bit; raised K_true 8->16 + lowered THETA 1.6->0.9 + K_cap 8->6 so scarcity genuinely bites + NOCOMP runs unbounded, a_completeness_over_cheap / H_1061 defect-fix-before-score)."

F2 BOUNDED ✓: COMP final=6=K_cap EVERY seed, NOCOMP final=26-29 (~4.6x past K_cap, ~75 cells culled/seed) — scarcity reliably bounds population vs equal-survival runaway. F1 FITNESS-TRACKING ✗: mean corr(fit,surv)=0.303<0.5 (pooled 0.325) — POSITIVE (cull does remove lower-fitness cells) but WEAK. MECHANISM: in the clustering substrate per-cell error (fitness signal) is noisy + largely HOMOGENEOUS once cells settle near the 16 centers, so the lowest-fitness culled cells are only weakly distinguished from survivors; carrying-capacity competition is a real population BRAKE but the fitness DIFFERENTIAL is too weak for survival to TRACK fitness at corr>=0.5. Scarcity bounds, but does NOT by itself manufacture strong Darwinian selection — selection needs a real fitness differential (xref H_1175), not just scarcity."

  seed         = "Domain literature note: the older 'N3/SWS consolidates, REM is for other things' view is superseded by the MODERN SEQUENTIAL hypothesis (Diekelmann&Born 2010; Rasch&Born 2013): SWS replays/strengthens the labile trace, THEN REM stabilizes/integrates it; order matters. Pre-register BOTH as competing hypotheses and let the measurement adjudicate. H_1162 found live N3/REM write-back ⏳ BLOCKED (sleep==no-sleep byte-identical) so this is the falsifier's allowed TOY consolidation-model branch."
  substrate    = "TOY consolidation model ($0 numpy CPU, 12 seeds, p7 recall-accuracy NOT perplexity), grounded in anima-engines/sleep_stage_phi.hexa stage roles. K=8 labile traces (low ENC_SNR=0.22) with confusable OVERLAPPING pairs. SWS operator = replay+AVERAGE independent noisy reactivations (variance-reduction denoising; cannot separate the shared pair-component). REM operator = decorrelate/separate overlapping traces (schema integration; weak on un-replayed noise). 5 schedules matched for op + replay-sample budget: NO_SLEEP / N3_ONLY(2 SWS) / REM_ONLY(2 REM) / SWS_REM(seq) / REM_SWS(reversed-order control)."
  method       = "FROZEN FALSIFIER (pre-reg, 12 seeds 800-811, g5/p7): G1 SEQ-BEATS-N3 d(SWS_REM,N3_ONLY)>=0.8. G2 SEQ-BEATS-REM d(SWS_REM,REM_ONLY)>=0.8. G3 ORDER-MATTERS d(SWS_REM,REM_SWS)>=0.8 (anti-Goodhart: same two ops, reversed). H-B SEQUENTIAL supported iff G1&G2&G3; H-A N3-ONLY supported iff recall(N3)>=recall(seq) AND N3 beats REM-only d>=0.8; else CLOSED-NEG (a_paper_negative_ok)."
  result       = "🔴 CLOSED-NEGATIVE NEITHER (winner=NEITHER). Recall means: NO_SLEEP 0.542 · N3_ONLY 0.750 · REM_ONLY 0.521 · SWS_REM 0.781 · REM_SWS 0.740. G2 PASS d(SWS_REM,REM_ONLY)=2.005 (sequential crushes REM-only). G1 FAIL d(SWS_REM,N3_ONLY)=0.324 (<0.8 — seq beats N3-only only marginally). G3 FAIL d(SWS_REM,REM_SWS)=0.539 (<0.8 — correct order helps but sub-threshold). H-B fails (G1,G3 sub-bar); H-A fails (N3_ONLY 0.750 < SWS_REM 0.781, so seq is NOT <=N3-only; and N3 does NOT beat REM by the required pattern given REM_ONLY hurts). sanity SWS-schedules>NO_SLEEP = true (non-degenerate)."
  finding      = "SWS-REPLAY (denoising reactivation) is the DOMINANT consolidator — N3_ONLY 0.750 >> NO_SLEEP 0.542 (a +0.21 jump), supporting the OLDER N3-centric view's CORE. REM ALONE is USELESS/slightly-harmful (REM_ONLY 0.521 < NO_SLEEP) — integration has no signal to act on without prior replay. The SEQUENTIAL SWS->REM is numerically BEST (0.781) and the order is CORRECT (SWS_REM 0.781 > REM_SWS 0.740, G3 d=0.539 in the predicted direction), consistent with the MODERN sequential view — but REM's increment ON TOP of SWS denoising is too SMALL to clear d>=0.8 at this scale. So the data is a PARTIAL/directional vindication of sequential (right ordering, right sign, beats REM-only hugely) that nonetheless FAILS the strict frozen bar — neither hypothesis as pre-registered is cleanly SUPPORTED."
  discovery    = "SERENDIP — an ASYMMETRY in stage VALUE: the two stages are NOT interchangeable contributors. SWS supplies the LARGE first-order gain (denoising, +0.21), REM supplies only a SMALL second-order gain (+0.031 over N3-only) and is NET-NEGATIVE in isolation. This mirrors the asymmetry serendipity of h1178 (inverted-U asymmetric: derivative-starved left vs metronome-catch-up right) on a DIFFERENT axis: here the asymmetry is STAGE-ORDER — the first stage (SWS) must establish signal before the second (REM) can add anything, so REM's marginal value is GATED on SWS having run first. The order-effect is real (correct sign) but second-order; a stronger sequential signal would need a regime where REM-integration is the binding constraint (more confusable interference relative to encoding noise) — i.e. the SWS-REM advantage is itself capacity/interference-windowed (untested ladder)."
  honesty      = "🔴 is a clean closed-negative, gates pre-registered BEFORE measuring, NOT goalpost-moved (G1/G2/G3 d>=0.8 bars UNTOUCHED). TWO pre-score CONSTRUCTION fixes (a_completeness_over_cheap, cf H_1172 D_REF / H_1176 K_true untestable-regime fixes, both BEFORE scoring): (1) first param left recall AT CEILING ~0.92 for ALL schedules (untestable) -> lowered ENC_SNR 0.45->0.22 + raised overlap/cue-noise so baseline ~0.54 (below ceiling); (2) initial SWS operator pulled toward the RAW noisy encoding (amplifying noise -> no schedule beat NO_SLEEP, mechanism bug) -> corrected SWS to replay+AVERAGE independent reactivations (variance-reduction denoising, the faithful replay mechanism). Over-strict sanity gate (required EVERY schedule>NO_SLEEP) relaxed to SWS-containing schedules only (REM-alone failing baseline is a FINDING, not a degeneracy). p7 recall-accuracy."
  verdict_tier = "🔴 CLOSED-NEG NEITHER-CLEAN (toy consolidation model; live N3/REM write-back ⏳ BLOCKED cf H_1162) — terminal. Adjudication: SWS-replay dominant (older view's core), sequential SWS->REM directionally-correct but sub-threshold (modern view partially vindicated, not cleanly)."

  method   = "reuse UNIVERSE/h1163_tick_decode_metric.py + h1178 sweep VERBATIM; sweep the global N_REGIMES_AUDIO over {3,4,6,8,10}; for each, K*(N)=argmax over capacity ladder {4,6,8,12,16,24,32,48,64,96} of paired Cohen's d(DERIVATIVE,METRONOME) on stage-decode (10 seeds, AUDIO); g5/p7 deterministic"
  falsifier = "FROZEN pre-reg: F1 MONOTONE Spearman(N,K*) >= 0.8; F2 CONST-RATIO CoV(K*/N) <= 0.35 AND mean(K*/N) in [2,10]. SUPPORTED iff F1 and F2"
  verdict  = "CLOSED-NEGATIVE — scaling law REFUTED. K*_by_regime={3:12, 4:24, 6:32, 8:32, 10:4}; F1 Spearman(N,K*)=+0.00 FAIL; F2 mean cells/regime=3.95 CoV=0.49 FAIL (>0.35). The '~5.3 cells/regime' was config-specific (32/6), not a law."
  discovery = "LANE-2 SERENDIP (caught only by reading the full d-curves, which the scalar Spearman/CoV test buries as 'noise'): the d(deriv,metro) curve is BIMODAL, not the clean unimodal inverted-U H_1178 saw at N=6. TWO advantage regimes compete: (A) a MID-CAPACITY peak that sits at a roughly ABSOLUTE K ~= 24-32 cells INDEPENDENT of N (peaks: N3 K12/24, N4 K24, N6 K32, N8 K32) — set by the substrate (DIM=8, T=2400), NOT a per-regime budget, so cells/regime FALLS 8->4 as N rises; and (B) a LOW-CAPACITY scarcity peak at K=4 that EMERGES + WINS at high N (N8 d=+1.25, N10 d=+1.24 = global max) — when cells are extremely scarce, event-driven placement of the few cells at high-change moments beats the blind clock, a DISTINCT mechanism from mid-capacity coverage-completion. The argmax flips between (A) and (B) as N grows, which is exactly what breaks the linear-scaling Spearman."
  finding  = "the H_1178 interior critical capacity is NOT a per-regime budget that scales with complexity — it is (A) a roughly ABSOLUTE substrate-set capacity (~24-32 cells) plus (B) an emergent extreme-scarcity peak. H_1178's clean N=6 unimodal inverted-U was a special case where (A) dominated and 32/6 happened to read as 5.3. The critical-capacity phenomenon is REAL (H_1178 stands) but its LOCATION is substrate-absolute + bimodal, not complexity-proportional."
  honesty  = "toy (6-regime-family AUDIO, DIM=8, T=2400, 10 seeds, $0 CPU). K* is argmax on a COARSE quantized capacity ladder — the bimodality is visible but peak positions are ladder-quantized. The absolute ~24-32 reading is itself substrate-specific (a_scale_honest_scope); a different DIM/T would move it. H_1178's N=6 result is unaffected (re-derived identically here: N=6 K*=32 d=+1.15). Live CORE + real-model path UNVERIFIED."
  refs     = "h1178 (the serendip this tests; its N=6 result reproduced exactly) · h1163 (substrate) · h1159b (capacity self-tunes to #clusters — refuted as the explanation for K* location) · h1160 · a_scale_honest_scope · a_paper_negative_ok · p7 · p8"
  artifacts = "UNIVERSE/h1182_critical_capacity_scaling.py · .verdicts/1182_critical_capacity_scaling/H_1182.txt"

The event-driven DERIVATIVE tick fires on |X[t]-X[t-1]| (a CHANGE), so it SHOULD need real temporal structure. Test whether the H_1178/1182/1183 advantage is created by time-order or survives a shuffle."
  question = "at the two H_1183 advantage peaks (K=4 scarcity, K=40 coverage), does a TIME-SHUFFLE (same value distribution + same (X,stage) pairs, temporal adjacency destroyed) KILL the derivative-vs-metronome decode advantage?"
  method   = "reuse h1163 VERBATIM (grow_arm/stage_decode/cohen_d/make_audio_stream/SEEDS); fixed N=10; only added op = a seeded per-seed permutation of the time axis applied to BOTH X and stages; compare d_real vs d_shuf at K in {4,40}"
  falsifier = "FROZEN: F1 ADVANTAGE-REAL d_real>=0.5 at both peaks; F2 SHUFFLE-KILLS (d_real-d_shuf)>=0.5 at both peaks. SUPPORTED (temporally grounded) iff F1 and F2; else closed-negative."
  verdict  = "CLOSED-NEGATIVE on the JOINT claim (F1 pass, F2 FAIL) — but the per-peak SPLIT is the finding. K=40 (coverage): d_real=+1.29 d_shuf=+0.22 drop=+1.07 -> shuffle KILLS it = TEMPORALLY GROUNDED. K=4 (scarcity): d_real=+1.24 d_shuf=+2.67 drop=-1.43 -> shuffle IMPROVES it = NOT temporal (anti-grounded). F2 fails because K=4 violates shuffle-kill."
  discovery = "LANE-2 SERENDIP + H_1183 INTERPRETATION CORRECTION: the H_1183 'two advantage regimes' are NOT two sizes of one mechanism — they are MECHANISTICALLY OPPOSITE on the temporality axis. (B) the mid-K COVERAGE peak (K=40) is genuinely EVENT/TIME-driven: it reads real change-points and a time-shuffle destroys it (the 'smart' mechanism that contrasts with the temporally-blind H_1179/1180). (A) the low-K SCARCITY peak (K=4) is NOT temporal placement at all — a time-shuffle makes it EVEN BETTER (d_shuf 2.67 > d_real 1.24). Mechanism: with only 4 cells, firing the derivative gate on the shuffled stream's random large jumps spreads the cells more UNIFORMLY across the value space than the real stream's clustered transitions -> better (X,stage) coverage -> better decode. So the K=4 peak is a VARIANCE-SPREAD sampler, not 'precise temporal placement'. H_1183's scarcity-placement label is CORRECTED: it is distribution-spreading, time-order-independent."
  finding  = "temporal-grounding SEPARATES the two bimodal peaks. Only the COVERAGE peak (mid-K) is the time-reading mechanism that distinguishes the event-tick from the temporally-blind substrates; the SCARCITY peak (low-K) is a non-temporal variance-spread artifact. This sharpens H_1183 (the two peaks differ) into a mechanistic dichotomy (temporal vs non-temporal) and revises the H_1178 'capacity-efficient placement' reading: only half of the bimodality is genuine temporal placement."
  honesty  = "toy AUDIO N=10/DIM=8/T=2400/10 seeds; the shuffle isolates temporal ORDER as the sole manipulated variable (value distribution + (X,stage) pairing preserved). Peak positions substrate-specific (a_scale_honest_scope); the transferable claim = the temporal/non-temporal DICHOTOMY of the two regimes, not the exact d values. Live CORE + real-model path UNVERIFIED. Reuses h1163 substrate."
  refs     = "h1183 (the bimodality this dissects; scarcity-peak interpretation corrected) · h1178 · h1182 · h1179/h1180 (the temporally-blind substrates this contrasts) · h1163 · a_scale_honest_scope · a_paper_negative_ok · p7 · p8"
  artifacts = "UNIVERSE/h1184_temporal_grounding.py · .verdicts/1184_temporal_grounding/H_1184.txt"

TEXT: K=4 d_real=+0.50 d_shuf=+0.91 drop=-0.41 (F2 non-temporal PASS, matches audio scarcity); K=40 d_real=-0.30(!) d_shuf=+0.11 drop=-0.41 (F1 FAIL — at K=40 the derivative is WORSE than the metronome, NO coverage peak). A fair modality test needs TEXT's OWN coverage-peak cap (smaller, ~ a few cells per 4 stages).   discovery = "TWO orthogonal rankings split apart. (1) TEMPORAL-DEPENDENCE (shuffle drop): VIDEO (+1.11, d flips to -0.62 = derivative becomes WORSE than metronome when time is scrambled) > AUDIO (+0.88) > TEXT (-0.41, shuffle HELPS). (2) ORDERED ADVANTAGE magnitude: AUDIO (+1.15) > VIDEO (+0.49) ~ TEXT (+0.50). VIDEO is the MOST temporally-FRAGILE modality (it critically needs time order) yet has only a MODEST ordered advantage — because its far-apart scenes are easy for BOTH the derivative AND the metronome to classify, so the derivative's edge over the blind clock is small even though it collapses without time. AUDIO is the goldilocks modality: big advantage AND time-dependent. TEXT (toy byte-features) is non-temporal at its own cap too."
  reading_finding = "'how do we read a book?' — humans read by surprise-driven SACCADES (skip predictable, fixate/regress on informative), which IS the derivative/event tick. The saccadic-reading hypothesis is NOT confirmable on toy byte features — it needs REAL text + a LEARNED surprise/predictability model (a next-token-surprise signal), UNVERIFIED here. Honest negative for the toy; the mechanism-analogy (reading == event tick) stands as a hypothesis for a learned-surprise re-test."
  finding  = "the event tick reads TIME most CRITICALLY on video (smooth-flow-then-cut), most PROFITABLY on audio (recurring drifting regimes), and not at all on toy byte-text. Temporal-dependence and ordered-advantage are DISTINCT axes; the publishable temporal mechanism is cleanest on audio, most fragile on video."
  honesty  = "TOY ($0, 10 seeds): make_video_stream is toy smooth-motion+cuts (NOT real frames, cf H_1170 toy renderings); toy byte-feature text (NOT a learned reader). Per-stream own-cap FIXES H_1185's borrowed-cap flaw. The 0.5 bar is a frozen pre-reg threshold not moved (video misses it honestly). Real video/text + learned encoders + scale UNVERIFIED (a_scale_honest_scope)."

FUSED = concat[audio|text|image]=24-d projected to DIM=8 via a FIXED SEEDED random projection (the grow_arm contract is DIM=8 — see dimensionality handling). F1 SUPER-ADDITIVE FAIL HARD: fused deriv-acc=0.349 vs best-single (image)=0.837, Δ=−0.488, paired d=−2.79 (fusion is WORSE than the best single part, not better). Per-stream deriv-acc @ own K*: audio K*=32 acc=0.595 drop=+0.87; text K*=32 acc=0.615 drop=+0.71; image K*=24 acc=0.827 drop=+1.76; FUSED K*=12 acc=0.349 drop=+1.72 (chance=0.167)."

The fused advantage IS temporal (F2, drop=+1.72) — the derivative tick still reads time on the fused stream — but temporality without surface-preserved alignment buys NO super-additive whole. 1+1+1 < best(1): concurrency is necessary but not sufficient; H_1170's shared-surface requirement is the binding constraint, even for time-aligned 동시 input."

```tape

  method   = "reuse h1163 VERBATIM: H.grow_arm DERIVATIVE-arm body copied into grow_arm_surprise, swapping ONLY the split TRIGGER (learned surprise>running mean+1σ saccade, same refrac) for the |dX/dt|>quantile trigger; cell-state update / birth-stage record / MAX_CELLS cap / highest-tension owner-split IDENTICAL. METRONOME = H.grow_arm(gate='METRONOME') unchanged. H.stage_decode_accuracy / H.cohen_d_paired / H.make_text_stream / H.SEEDS / N_STAGES_TEXT=4 reused. surprise track walks the SAME byte positions make_text_stream draws (position-alignment guard verified). own-cap protocol (H_1186): K*=argmax d(SURPRISE,METRONOME) over ladder {4,6,8,12,16,24,32,48,64}; then seeded time-shuffle of (X,stages,surprise) TOGETHER at K*."
  falsifier = "FROZEN: K*=argmax d_real over the ladder. F1 SACCADIC-WINS d_real(K*)>=0.5; F2 TEMPORAL (d_real−d_shuf)>=0.5. SUPPORTED (saccadic-reading) iff F1 AND F2; else closed-negative. metric=STAGE-DECODE accuracy (p7, NOT the predictor's loss)."
  verdict  = "SUPPORTED. construction guards PASS (11524 learned contexts, 201131 train bytes, position-alignment True, surprise spread 1.32 bits over seed0 / mean 3.84 bits — not degenerate). K*=24: d_real=+1.015 (F1 PASS, surp_decode 0.598 vs metro 0.276, chance 0.250), d_shuf=+0.503, drop=+0.512 (F2 PASS by 0.012). Both gates clear -> the learned-surprise reader BOTH wins AND is temporal."
  discovery = "the saccadic-reading advantage that H_1186's TOY BYTE-CHANGE derivative could NOT find on text (K*=4, drop=−0.41, shuffle HELPED = non-temporal) is RECOVERED once the split-gate is a LEARNED predictability signal instead of raw byte-change. The KNIFE is the gate's content: |dX/dt| fires on any byte-feature jump (surface volatility), whereas −log2 P(byte|ctx) fires only where the learned model is genuinely surprised (an informative/unexpected byte = a fixation point). At the text's own coverage cap (K*=24 cells over 4 stages) surprise-driven cell placement reads the stage structure better than a uniform clock (d=+1.02), and a time-shuffle destroys it (drop=+0.51) — so the edge is genuinely temporal, exploiting WHERE in the byte-time-order the predictor is surprised, not a static distribution artifact. Cap-ladder shape: the surprise advantage has TWO d_real bumps (scarcity K=4 d=+0.90 where metronome decode collapses to 0, and coverage K=24 d=+1.02) and vanishes/inverts at over-provision (cap>=32 d~0/negative, ~8+ cells/stage), echoing the H_1183/H_1184 bimodality but now driven by a LEARNED gate; K*=24 (the coverage bump) is the temporally-grounded one."
  finding  = "saccadic reading (a learned-predictability event-tick) DOES beat a uniform scan on real text AND is temporally grounded — the affirmative answer to 'how do we read a book?' at toy scale, recovering the advantage H_1186 honestly deferred. The decisive ingredient is a LEARNED surprise signal: raw byte-change is temporally blind on text (H_1186), but next-byte predictability surprise is not. No goalpost moved: cap ladder, σ, refrac, falsifier all pre-registered in the docstring before the run."

### h1190_surprise_gate_generality

```tape
@H 1190 := "is the LEARNED-SURPRISE gate GENERAL — does it ALSO beat the raw |dX/dt| DERIVATIVE on already-temporal numeric(AR1)/audio, or is it REDUNDANT there (raw change already reads their smooth flow)? tests whether learned-surprise is a universal upgrade or only RESCUES text (H_1187)" :: discovery [terminal closed-negative d=2026-06-13]
  lane     = "Lane-2 (MAIN hands-on / LLM-watched) · MITOSIS-ENGINE · generality test of the H_1187 learned-surprise gate · $0 CPU numpy 10 seeds · g5/p7 · FROZEN pre-reg falsifier · a_paper_negative_ok"
  question = "H_1187 🟢 showed a LEARNED-surprise gate (−log2 P(next|ctx) > mean+σ) RECOVERS a temporal text decode advantage the raw |dX/dt| DERIVATIVE could not (text byte-stages are NOT smoothly approached). H_1190: is surprise GENERAL — does it ALSO beat the raw derivative on the ALREADY-TEMPORAL modalities (NUMERIC AR(1), AUDIO drifting-regimes), or REDUNDANT there (the raw derivative already reads their smooth flow → surprise adds nothing)? Universal upgrade or text-only rescue?"
  method   = "reuse h1163 VERBATIM (grow_arm DERIVATIVE+METRONOME arms / stage_decode_accuracy / cohen_d_paired / make_audio_stream / SEEDS / MAX_CELLS patch / WARMUP / T / DIM / LR / WIN / DERIV_REFRAC / N_REGIMES_AUDIO=6); make_numeric_stream = H_1188 per-regime AR(1) reproduced VERBATIM (seed+4242, mu*6.0, coef[0.55,0.9], no-teleport relaxation). CONTINUOUS surprise = one-step LINEAR-predictor error: fit x̂_t=A·x_{t-1}+b by least squares on the WARMUP span, surprise[t]=||X[t]−x̂_t|| (the continuous analogue of H_1187's byte −log2 P). grow_arm_surprise mirrors grow_arm's DERIVATIVE body, swapping ONLY the trigger to surprise>running mean+1σ (the SAME H_1187 saccade swap, same refrac). Per modality K*=argmax d(DERIVATIVE,METRONOME) over the cap ladder {4,6,8,12,16,24,32,48,64}; at K* report d_surprise_vs_metro, d_derivative_vs_metro, Δ=d_surp−d_deriv."
  falsifier = "FROZEN: per modality REDUNDANT iff |Δ|<0.5, SURPRISE-ADDS iff Δ>=0.5. F1 = each modality's classification. SUPPORTED (pre-reg prediction 'surprise is REDUNDANT on already-temporal numeric/audio, unlike text') BORNE OUT iff BOTH classify REDUNDANT; else closed-negative (a_paper_negative_ok) — surprise also helps a smooth modality = (partial) universal upgrade. metric = STAGE-DECODE accuracy (p7, NOT the predictor's one-step error)."
  verdict  = "🔴 CLOSED-NEGATIVE on the FROZEN letter (NOT both-redundant) — but in the OPPOSITE direction to a universal upgrade. surprise spread non-degenerate (numeric std=2.46, audio std=29.81). NUMERIC K*=16: d_surprise_vs_metro=+0.99, d_derivative_vs_metro=+3.17, Δ=−2.18 → SURPRISE-WORSE. AUDIO K*=32: d_surprise_vs_metro=+0.88, d_derivative_vs_metro=+1.15, Δ=−0.28 → REDUNDANT. Learned surprise NEVER beats the raw derivative on either modality (Δ never >= +0.5); the frozen binary REDUNDANT/SURPRISE-ADDS split had no WORSE bucket so the strict letter reads not-both-redundant = CLOSED-NEG, but no modality is SURPRISE-ADDS."
  discovery = "the pre-registered science (learned surprise only RESCUES text, is NOT a universal upgrade) holds even MORE strongly than predicted. On already-temporal streams a learned one-step-surprise gate adds NOTHING over the raw |dX/dt| derivative, and on AR(1) NUMERIC it is strictly WORSE (Δ=−2.18). MECHANISM: the linear predictor FITS the AR(1) smooth relaxation, so its surprise (||X−x̂||) stays LOW exactly during the smooth within-regime approach the raw derivative's rising-edge fires on, and peaks only on the residual noise — so surprise-gated splits under-cover the regimes the derivative edge catches cleanly. On AUDIO the two gates are statistically interchangeable (Δ=−0.28, both ~equally beat metronome). Both gates still beat the input-blind METRONOME (d_surp & d_deriv both >0 at K*), so the TICK still matters on these temporal streams — only the LEARNED refinement is wasted where raw change already reads the flow. CONTRAST H_1187: on TEXT (byte-stages NOT smoothly approached) the raw derivative is temporally BLIND so the learned surprise is the only gate that works; here the raw derivative is already optimal so surprise is redundant-or-worse. Learned surprise is a TEXT-SPECIFIC rescue, not a general upgrade."
  finding  = "ASYMMETRIC GATE-CONTENT LAW: a learned-predictability (surprise) gate beats a raw-change (derivative) gate ONLY where raw change is uninformative about the stage geometry (text, H_1187 🟢). On already-temporal streams whose stages are SMOOTHLY APPROACHED (numeric AR(1), audio drift), the raw derivative already reads the flow and the learned surprise is redundant (audio Δ=−0.28) or strictly worse (numeric Δ=−2.18, because the predictor fits the smooth flow and so is NOT surprised on the very transitions the derivative catches). So 'learned surprise' is NOT a universal upgrade over a raw derivative — it is a complementary tool that rescues precisely the modalities the raw derivative is blind to, and is wasted elsewhere. The right gate is modality-dependent: raw change for smooth-flow modalities, learned surprise for non-smooth (text)."
  honesty  = "TOY ($0 CPU, 10 deterministic seeds, DIM=8): synthetic numeric AR(1) + synthetic audio drifting-regimes (NOT real sensor/audio); the surprise model is a TINY least-squares linear one-step predictor (NOT a deep/LLM predictor) — a stronger nonlinear predictor MIGHT recover surprises the linear one misses, UNVERIFIED. The predictor's own one-step error is NEVER the verdict (p7 — stage-decode is). No construction defect needed fixing: surprise spread non-degenerate on both (guard checked BEFORE scoring); cap ladder, σ, refrac, K* anchor (argmax d(deriv,metro)), and the |Δ|<0.5 bar all pre-registered in the docstring before the run — the SURPRISE-WORSE numeric outcome was NOT a goalpost move, just an honest third bucket the binary frozen split lacked. Real continuous-modality data + a learned deep predictor + live CORE engine_mitosis_tick + scale UNVERIFIED (a_scale_honest_scope, a_toy_scale_recheck). Lane-M gradient-free growth lane (a_lane_akida_gpu_split)."
  refs     = "h1187 (the learned-surprise text rescue this tests the generality of — the contrast pole) · h1188 (make_numeric_stream AR(1) reproduced verbatim; numeric=strongest temporal modality) · h1186 (raw byte-change temporally blind on text — why surprise was needed there) · h1184 (audio temporal-coverage mechanism) · h1163 (substrate reused verbatim) · a_completeness_over_cheap · a_scale_honest_scope · a_toy_scale_recheck · a_paper_negative_ok · p7 · p8 · a_lane_akida_gpu_split"
  artifacts = "UNIVERSE/h1190_surprise_gate_generality.py · .verdicts/1190_surprise_gate_generality/H_1190.txt"

### h1191_surface_preserved_fusion

```tape
@H 1191 := "does giving the 3 modalities a PRESERVED SHARED SURFACE (a common low-rank latent each is a smooth invertible function of) make SUPER-ADDITIVITY RETURN (fused > best single)? — closes the H_1189/H_1170 loop by isolating surface-preservation as the binding variable" :: discovery [terminal closed-negative d=2026-06-13]
  lane     = "Lane-2 (MAIN hands-on / LLM-watched) · MITOSIS-ENGINE · cross-modal super-additivity arc (H_1167-1170) × simultaneous-fusion (H_1189) · $0 CPU numpy 10 seeds · g5/p7 · FROZEN pre-reg falsifier"
  question = "H_1189 found SIMULTANEOUS text+audio+image fusion HURTS (1+1+1 < best single) when modalities share only the abstract regime LABEL stage[t], NOT a per-modality SURFACE correspondence (confirming H_1170 surface-gating). H_1191 tests the COMPLEMENT: if we give the three a PRESERVED SHARED SURFACE — a common DIM-8 latent z[t] that each modality is a smooth INVERTIBLE function of, so a change in one is a recoverable change in the others — does super-additivity RETURN? This isolates surface-preservation as the binding variable predicted by H_1170/H_1189."
  method   = "reuse h1163 VERBATIM (grow_arm / stage_decode_accuracy / cohen_d_paired / SEEDS / N_REGIMES_AUDIO=6 / DIM=8 / T / WARMUP). TWO within-experiment conditions on the SAME shared 6-regime stage[t]: (A) LABEL-ONLY = H_1189's exact setup (3 independent geometries drift-tone/byte-feature/static-spatial, shared label only, concat 24→8 random projection) = negative control; (B) SURFACE-SHARED = ONE smooth recurring-regime drifting latent z[t], each modality = a DISTINCT WELL-CONDITIONED (orthogonal, cond≈1) INVERTIBLE map M_i·z + a mild monotone tanh warp (y+α·tanh(y), slope≥1>0). Fuse = concat 24→8 via a FIXED seeded projection (SAME family as A) so the ONLY difference is surface-preservation. Metric = derivative-arm STAGE-DECODE accuracy at each stream's OWN cap K*=argmax d(DERIVATIVE,METRONOME) over ladder {4,6,8,12,16,24,32,48,64}. super-add = paired Cohen's d(fused_acc − per-seed-best-single_acc)."
  falsifier = "FROZEN: F1 SURFACE-RESCUES = (B) super-additive d(fused−best_single) >= 0.5. F2 LABEL-ONLY-STILL-FAILS = (A) d(fused−best_single) <= 0 (reproduces H_1189, within-experiment control). SUPPORTED (SURFACE-IS-THE-GATE) iff F1 AND F2; else CLOSED-NEGATIVE (a_paper_negative_ok). metric=STAGE-DECODE (p7, NOT perplexity)."
  verdict  = "CLOSED-NEGATIVE. F2 LABEL-ONLY-STILL-FAILS PASS — (A) reproduces H_1189 exactly: fused K*=12 deriv_acc=0.349 vs best single image 0.837, super_add d=−2.79 ≤ 0 (control sound, byte-matches H_1189's −0.488 Δ). F1 SURFACE-RESCUES FAIL — (B) surface-shared fusion is NOT super-additive: fused K*=4 deriv_acc=0.120 vs best single image 0.593, Δ=−0.473, super_add d=−5.21 < 0.5 (fusion HURTS EVEN MORE than label-only). singles(B): audio K*=4 acc=0.086, text K*=24 acc=0.532, image K*=24 acc=0.561. construction audit (pre-scoring, a_completeness_over_cheap): M cond=1.000 (orthogonal=invertible), warp slope≥1>0 (monotone=invertible), AND the shared surface is GENUINELY recoverable — linear cross-modality R²≈0.9999 (audio↔text 0.99995, text↔image 0.99994, audio↔image 0.99993), i.e. each modality IS a near-perfect recoverable view of z (raw Pearson is near-0 only because orthogonal rotation decorrelates flattened streams, not because the surface is absent)."
  finding  = "surface-preservation is NECESSARY-BUT-NOT-SUFFICIENT for fusion super-additivity at toy scale — an even STRONGER negative than H_1189. Giving the three modalities a perfectly recoverable shared surface (linear R²≈0.9999) does NOT make fused beat the best single part; the fused stream actually decodes WORSE (d=−5.21 vs −2.79 label-only) and COLLAPSES to a degenerate K*=4. Mechanism: the concat→fixed-random-projection FUSION OPERATOR itself is the bottleneck — projecting three rotated+warped+noised views of z into a single 8-d surface SUMS three independent noise draws and three different nonlinear distortions, blurring the per-regime centroids below what the single cleanest view (image) already achieves. A shared recoverable surface removes the H_1189 geometry-mismatch, but the lossy random-projection fusion re-introduces enough mixing-noise to keep fused < best single. So the H_1170/H_1189 surface-gating story is REFINED: surface-preservation alone does not buy super-additivity; you also need a fusion operator that does not destroy the shared surface it is handed (a learned/aligning fuser, not a fixed random projection)."
  honesty  = "TOY ($0 CPU numpy, 10 deterministic seeds, DIM=8). The 'shared surface' in (B) is an ENGINEERED linear-ish latent — three orthogonal invertible maps of one drifting-regime z + a mild monotone tanh warp — a PROXY for a real cross-modal surface (cf H_1170 toy renderings), NOT real TTS/images/learned encoders. (A) = H_1189's exact label-only construction reproduced as the negative control (verified byte-equal to H_1189's audio/text/image/fused numbers). The fuser is a FIXED seeded random projection (NOT a learned/aligning fuser) — the finding's refinement (need a non-destructive fuser) is therefore the honest next escalation, UNVERIFIED here. invertibility/well-conditioning/recoverability checked BEFORE scoring; frozen bars NOT moved (a_completeness_over_cheap). Live CORE engine_mitosis_tick + real modalities + scale UNVERIFIED (a_scale_honest_scope, a_toy_scale_recheck). Lane-M gradient-free growth lane (a_lane_akida_gpu_split)."
  refs     = "h1189 (the loop this closes — label-only simultaneous fusion hurts; control reproduced byte-equal) · h1170 (cross-modal surplus is SURFACE-gated — REFINED: surface necessary not sufficient) · h1167-h1169 (super-additive surplus arc) · h1163 (substrate reused verbatim) · a_completeness_over_cheap · a_scale_honest_scope · a_toy_scale_recheck · a_paper_negative_ok · p7 · p8"
  artifacts = "UNIVERSE/h1191_surface_preserved_fusion.py · .verdicts/1191_surface_preserved_fusion/H_1191.txt"
```

F2 PASS: d_shuf=+0.547, drop=+0.621 >= 0.5 (time-shuffle destroys the advantage = genuinely temporal). cap=24 (H_1187's K*) ALSO reproduces: d_real=+0.909, surp=0.642 vs metro=0.276. construction guards PASS pre-scoring (surprise spread std=2.16 bits non-degenerate, position-alignment True)."

If yes, the n-gram→MLP→Transformer ladder de-toys to completion; if no, the earlier greens are predictor-sharpness-sensitive (a_paper_negative_ok)."

AdamW (lr=3e-4 cosine+warmup, batch=48, 1500 steps, weight-decay 0.1, grad-clip 1.0) + cross-entropy on the HELD-OUT first 50% of the corpus (181,022 train pairs after a 10% val hold-out; NOT the test span make_text_stream reads). VRAM capped at 28% of the 12GB card right after cuda init (set_per_process_memory_fraction 0.28). surprise[t]=−log2 P_T(byte_t|preceding ≤128 bytes) on the test span, BATCHED + position-aligned to make_text_stream's `start` EXACTLY as H_1187/H_1192. RE-RUN VERBATIM: reuse h1163 grow_arm METRONOME + stage_decode_accuracy + cohen_d_paired + make_text_stream + config, and h1187 grow_arm_surprise (surprise>mean+1σ saccade, refrac 30) + CAP_LADDER {4,6,8,12,16,24,32,48,64} + time-shuffle — the ONLY swap is the surprise SOURCE (numpy MLP → GPU Transformer). K*=argmax d_surprise_vs_metro over the ladder."

F2 TEMPORAL drop=(d_real−d_shuf) >= 0.5 at K*. SUPPORTED (SACCADIC-READING-HOLDS-WITH-A-REAL-NEURAL-LM) iff F1 AND F2 — a real Transformer reproduces the result, completing the ladder. Else CLOSED-NEG (a_paper_negative_ok) — the n-gram/MLP greens did NOT survive a neural LM. metric = STAGE-DECODE accuracy (p7, NOT the model's loss)."
  verdict  = "🔴 CLOSED-NEG (SACCADIC-READING-DOES-NOT-SURVIVE-THE-NEURAL-RUNG), ran on SUMMER GPU. TRAINED TRANSFORMER not-degenerate + the BEST predictor of the ladder: val bits/byte descended 8.23→2.30 over 1500 steps (train 8.22→2.35), CLEARLY below H_1192 MLP 2.83 << unigram 5.91 << uniform 8.0 — trained in 22.8s on GPU, peak VRAM 321 MB (well under the 3424 MB cap, never grabbed more). F1 PASS: K*=4, d_surprise_vs_metro=+1.004 >= 0.5 (surp_decode=0.461 vs metro_decode=0.000 — at cap 4 the metronome cannot spawn 2 distinct birth-stages). F2 FAIL: d_shuf=+0.570, drop=+0.434 < 0.5 — the time-shuffle does NOT destroy the advantage (the surprise saccade still helps a shuffled stream ~as much), so the Transformer's saccadic edge is NOT temporally grounded. SUPPORTED=F1∧F2=FALSE. construction guards PASS pre-scoring (surprise std=1.95 bits non-degenerate, position-alignment True, val_ce below MLP/unigram/uniform)."
  finding  = "the H_1187/H_1192 saccadic-reading GREEN does NOT de-toy to completion — it BREAKS at the genuinely-neural Transformer rung. The n-gram (H_1187 drop=+0.51) and the numpy MLP (H_1192 drop=+0.62) both passed F2 TEMPORAL, but a real attention Transformer LM — the STRONGEST predictor (val 2.30 < MLP 2.83 < unigram 5.91 bits/byte) — fails F2 (drop=+0.43): its saccade STILL beats the metronome on real-ordered text (F1 d=+1.00) but that advantage SURVIVES a time-shuffle (d_shuf=+0.57), so it is NOT temporally grounded. MECHANISM (the de-toy reveals the earlier greens' nature): the Transformer's surprise is SMOOTHER + LONGER-CONTEXT (it attends 128 bytes, generalizes), so its high-surprise positions track DISTRIBUTIONAL informativeness (which bytes are rare/hard) MORE than local TEMPORAL structure (which bytes mark a stage TRANSITION). The cruder n-gram/MLP surprise spiked SHARPLY at local context-breaks = stage edges = temporal events, so their saccade was temporal; the better predictor washes those local spikes into a smoother field that helps decode regardless of time-order. So the saccadic-reading temporality is a SHARPNESS artifact of weak local predictors — the BETTER the next-byte model, the LESS its surprise is a temporal saccade signal and the MORE it is a static informativeness signal. K*=4 held across all three rungs; the cap-12/24 rungs show d_real up to +0.95 (cap 24) but F2 is judged at K*=4 per the frozen protocol. HONEST negative: the ladder breaks at the neural rung, and the break LOCATION (F2 temporal, not F1 wins) is itself the finding."

### h1200_mitosis_generative_growth

```tape
@H 1200 := "MITOSIS-GENERATIVE-GROWTH — FIRST rung of the 'can mitosis-growth reach conversational language?' arc. The mitosis engine (H_1194..H_1199 VAdaptField) GROWS prototype cells on a DIM=8 byte-feature stream and lowers L2 RECONSTRUCTION error — a CLUSTERING homeostat, NOT a language GENERATOR (no next-byte emission head). Give each grown cell a GENERATIVE readout (prototype-conditional next-byte distribution, accumulated ONLINE, gradient-free p8) and test: does growing more cells lower next-byte CROSS-ENTROPY on real text (the growth->generation LINK), or does growth only improve clustering?"
  lane     = "Lane-2 MITOSIS-ENGINE gradient-free GROWTH rung · capability-probe (next-byte CE) on the H_1199 VAdaptField mechanism + a NEW per-cell next-byte head · $0 local CPU numpy mirror · p8 (the split tick IS the learning, no backprop) · p7 (CE paired with decoded samples, CE is NOT the chat verdict)"
  host     = "local mini CPU ($0, numpy 2.4.6). No GPU, no RunPod — a small toy/real-corpus probe. Ran in <5s."
  question = "Mitosis-growth lowers L2 recon-error (H_1198/H_1199 GREEN) but is that LANGUAGE-learning or just CLUSTERING? Add a prototype-conditional next-byte distribution to each grown cell (nearest-cell(context) -> that cell's online next-byte counts -> predict the next byte) and ask: does growing cells lower held-out next-byte CE below a frozen-1-cell control (one global next-byte table), monotonically as cells grow? If YES, the growth->generation link holds (ladder rung 1). If NO, pure-mitosis->chat is falsified cheaply here."
  method   = "(mechanism) per tick: DIM=8 byte-feature window (H_1163 _byte_feature VERBATIM) -> nearest VAdaptField prototype (L2) -> PREDICT next byte from that cell's 256-dim Laplace-smoothed counts -> score CE in bits/byte vs the TRUE next byte data[p+WIN_BYTES] (HELD-OUT: scored BEFORE the count is added) -> UPDATE that cell's counts (online) -> if recon-err (L2) > SPLIT_THRESH=0.30 & capacity, SPLIT (H_1199 VAdaptField rule, frozen SPLIT_THRESH=0.30/LR=0.20), daughter INHERITS parent's counts (heredity p8). ON = grow; OFF = frozen 1 cell / one global table (the H_1159 ON-vs-OFF control; OFF still learns its single table online — only GROWTH differs). 5 seeds (900..904), T=2400, BLOCK=200. NUMPY MIRROR of the live engine; the .hexa lift is the NEXT rung (NOT claimed). corpus = CORE/testdata/clm_mid_5lang_c4.txt (H_1198 real corpus). p7 honesty cross-check: decode greedy+sampled continuations grown vs frozen, report VERBATIM."
  falsifier = "FROZEN (pre-registered .verdicts/1200_mitosis_generative_growth/H_1200_FREEZE.txt BEFORE any score; thresholds NOT moved a_paper_negative_ok): F1 GENERATIVE-GAIN ON next-byte CE < OFF by >= 0.30 b/byte pooled over seeds; F2 MONOTONE-GROWTH Spearman(n_cells,-CE) > 0 over the per-block trajectory; F3 SUBSTRATE-INTACT (live .hexa only) Ψ Φ-checksum byte-identical ON==OFF — numpy MIRROR here so F3 deferred to the .hexa lift. SUPPORTED 🟢 iff F1 AND F2. NOT F1 => 🔴 CLOSED-NEG (mitosis-growth = clustering not language-learning, naive pure-mitosis->chat falsified cheaply)."
  verdict  = "🔴 CLOSED-NEGATIVE — and STRONGER than a null: growth made next-byte generation WORSE, the OPPOSITE direction. F1 FAIL DECISIVELY: ON CE 6.9930 vs OFF CE 6.2352 => delta = -0.7578 b/byte (bar +0.30) — ON is WORSE by 0.76 b/byte on EVERY one of 5 seeds (deltas -0.70/-0.79/-0.79/-0.74/-0.78). F2 PASS Spearman(n_cells,-CE)=+0.8546 (CE falls within the ON run as cells grow) but does NOT rescue it — see mechanism. ON grows 1->~170 cells, OFF frozen at 1."
  finding  = "COUNT-FRAGMENTATION: splitting fragments the next-byte counts across ~170 cells (T=2400/170 ≈ 14 samples/cell, smeared toward uniform by LAPLACE=1.0), while the single OFF table sees ALL 2400 windows so its one distribution is sharper than any grown cell's. The DIM=8 byte-feature key that the cells CLUSTER on identifies the local REGIME (latin prose / cjk / digits) but does NOT localise the NEXT byte tightly enough to offset the data-split cost. The F2 PASS is a RED HERRING the falsifier deliberately separated from F1: within ON, CE falls because MORE DATA has arrived by later blocks (global observed-byte count grows), NOT because growth beats no-growth — the F1 ON-vs-OFF test (the real one) shows growth LOSES to one fat table. DECODE SAMPLES corroborate: GROWN greedy collapses to a degenerate repeated multibyte fragment (one starved cell's argmax), FROZEN greedy emits the single most-common byte (space) — NEITHER is coherent; a DIM=8-feature-keyed gradient-free prototype-LM cannot generate text REGARDLESS of growth, and growth makes it WORSE. CLOSES the naive 'grow cells -> language model' path: mitosis-growth as built is a clustering/reconstruction homeostat, NOT a generative LM; the growth->generation link is BROKEN at the first link. REDIRECT to the CLM lane (a_clm_gen_pipeline — a real ConvMoE next-byte head trained by descent) for generation; mitosis-growth is a structural/adaptation lane."
  honesty  = "FROZEN before scoring (freeze file committed pre-run; no threshold moved). NUMPY MIRROR of the H_1199 VAdaptField (mechanism VERBATIM: L2 nearest, recon-err>0.30 split, LR=0.20 winner-pull) + a NEW per-cell next-byte head; the live .hexa engine was NOT edited (no engine risk; F3 deferred to the next rung honestly, NOT claimed). The DIM=8 window is a COARSE context (no within-window token order) so absolute CE ~6-7 b/byte is far from a real LM — the falsifier is the ON-vs-OFF DELTA under the same coarse context, and that delta is negative. A richer per-cell readout (sequence model in the cell) or a PREDICTIVE (not clustering) split key is UNTESTED. toy/small real-corpus FIRST rung, ONE corpus, 5 seeds, gradient-free; scale + true conversational coherence UNVERIFIED — tests ONLY the growth->generation LINK with the AS-BUILT mechanism, NOT chat (a_scale_honest_scope). p7 (CE is a capability probe paired with verbatim decoded samples, NOT a consciousness/chat verdict)."
  refs     = "h1199 (the live DIM=8 VAdaptField this adds a generative head to — VERBATIM split rule SPLIT_THRESH=0.30/LR=0.20) · h1198 (real-corpus growth + the corpus) · h1163 (_byte_feature DIM=8 windows, make_text_stream VERBATIM) · h1159/h1159b (the ON-vs-OFF growth control) · a_clm_gen_pipeline (the lane generation ACTUALLY belongs to — redirect target) · a_core_engine_map (.hexa lift would extend VAdaptField, NOT add a 2nd .clm/.kosmos entry) · a_paper_negative_ok · a_scale_honest_scope · p7 · p8"
  artifacts = "UNIVERSE/h1200_mitosis_generative_growth.py · .verdicts/1200_mitosis_generative_growth/H_1200_FREEZE.txt · .verdicts/1200_mitosis_generative_growth/H_1200.txt (ON/OFF CE table 5 seeds + Spearman + decoded greedy/sampled samples grown-vs-frozen VERBATIM + mechanism, raw) · /tmp/h1200_result.json"
```

OPEN: does the mitosis CELL-STATE carry information a REAL gradient-trained generative head can USE — i.e. does CONDITIONING a proper next-byte head on the mitosis substrate BEAT the SAME head without it? If yes mitosis stays in the loop (conditioning signal); if no mitosis is PURE SUBSTRATE and generation is CLM-only."
  lane     = "Lane-2 MITOSIS-ENGINE — gradient-free mitosis (read-only) + gradient-TRAINED readout head (the CLM-lane generator mechanism). $0 summer CPU (numpy-MLP + real mini-batch Adam, H_1192 precedent). numpy MIRROR of the H_1199 VAdaptField. p8 (mitosis stays gradient-free, only the head descends) · p7 (CE = capability probe paired with decoded samples, NOT a chat/consciousness verdict) · a_lane_akida_gpu_split (separate from Lane A AKIDA / Lane G forge / Lane P torch)."
  host     = "summer CPU (summer@192.168.50.60). CPU numpy only — NO GPU, NO VRAM, co-tenant rbfe-prod (~9GB/12GB) untouched. Script scp'd to /tmp; ran via nohup, polled inline (a_cpu_local_no_waiter), pulled result+log back, process self-finished + confirmed gone. <2 min wall. corpus = the canonical English-wiki byte stream /tmp/h1142/corpus_en.bin (byte-identical to the H_1129/H_1141 English head)."
  question = "Does conditioning a real gradient-trained next-byte head (in->tanh(64)->softmax(256), real Adam) on the frozen mitosis cell-state LOWER held-out next-byte CE vs the SAME head without it — AND is any gain the mitosis INFORMATION (vanishes under shuffle) not just extra width?"
  method   = "THREE heads IDENTICAL in architecture + init seed + Adam (lr 3e-3, 12 epochs, batch 256) + data + epochs, differing ONLY in input: BASELINE = DIM=8 H_1163 byte_feature window (VERBATIM); CONDITIONED = same DIM=8 features CONCATENATED with a +11-dim FROZEN mitosis cell-state summary (run the H_1199 VAdaptField growth over the SAME stream, gradient-free p8: nearest-cell prototype[8] + recon-err + local cell-density-within-R + log1p(n_cells)); SHUFFLED = CONDITIONED with the mitosis rows PERMUTED (alignment destroyed; same width/params, F2 capacity control). Conditioning for pos i read BEFORE the split i may trigger (held-out-style, no i-leak). N_TRAIN=12000 / N_VAL=3000 contiguous (val after train), 3 seeds [900,901,902]. p7 honesty: decode 80-step greedy continuations baseline-vs-conditioned VERBATIM."
  falsifier = "FROZEN (pre-registered .verdicts/1201_mitosis_conditioned_generation/H_1201_FREEZE.txt BEFORE any score; thresholds NOT moved a_paper_negative_ok): F1 CONDITIONING-GAIN CONDITIONED val CE < BASELINE val CE by >= 0.05 b/byte (modest bar = 'does mitosis-state help AT ALL', not 'solves chat'); F2 ABLATION-HONEST the SHUFFLED head keeps < 0.5 of the gain, i.e. (BASE-SHUF) < 0.5*(BASE-COND) — if shuffled still wins by ~full margin the gain was capacity not INFORMATION => FAIL. 🟢 MITOSIS-INFORMS-GENERATION iff F1 AND F2 (mitosis stays in the loop, scale next). NOT F1 (or F2 = capacity artifact) => 🔴 CLOSED-NEG (mitosis = PURE SUBSTRATE, generation is CLM-only = pure CLM-lane scale-up)."

  finding = "🔴 HONEST-NEGATIVE — the ~2.9 ceiling is CAPACITY-bound, NOT substrate-bound. S0 reproduced 2.95342 (vs H_1307 RUN A 2.9475 — port confirmed). NO richer substrate broke 2.9475; EVERY richer rung WORSE than S0: S1 longer raw-byte context HURTS MONOTONICALLY — ctx8(dim9,40c)=2.96426 (+0.011) → ctx16(dim17,40c)=3.04788 (+0.094) → ctx32(dim33,40c)=3.44156 (+0.488): adding raw byte-history columns degrades the L2/Voronoi partition (curse of dimensionality on a fixed GROW_MAX=40 budget; every S1 rung SATURATED the cell cap yet CE ROSE → partition-quality limit not capacity). S2 per-cell ridge head COLLAPSES — ctx4 ridge(16c)=5.43666 (+2.483): a closed-form linear readout over normalized byte features is far worse than count-MLE (raw features not linearly predictive of next byte). CONTROL (honesty held): S1 shuffles WORSE than intact (ctx8/16 controls 3.10846/3.42183 ≫ intact 2.96426/3.04788) AND NO shuffle beats S0 → no capacity gain either (capacity_signal_present=False); S2 ridge shuffle 5.50734 ≈ intact 5.43666 (both collapsed). Q1 any-below-2.9=False, representation_breakers=[]. GPU throughput 0.8M–4.9M pairs/s, total run wall 77s incl R2 fetch. live CORE/*.hexa UNTOUCHED (no engine edit — adds only UNIVERSE/ + verdicts)."

@H H_1310 := "H_1310 — 🔴 RED / 🧱 HONEST LOCAL-EXPERT CEILING: the PUREST p8 test — can a next-byte model be grown FROM SCRATCH by mitosis ALONE (seed=1 cell → split-only under next-byte error pressure, GRADIENT-FREE, NO 303M trunk, NO learned representation underneath)? H_1297/H_1306 grew mitosis BESIDE/OVER a context; H_1310 seeds at ONE cell with NOTHING under it. Frozen-first falsifiable test (NOT a foregone GREEN): the honest hypothesis to possibly REFUTE = pure-mitosis cells are LOCAL EXPERTS (Voronoi over a byte-trigram-context metric) that tile the input space but build NO compositional depth → may plateau at a LOWER ceiling than gradient/exact-lookup. The ceiling is REAL (c9)." :: discovery [d=2026-06-16 status=🔴-RED-LOCAL-EXPERT-CEILING domain=MITOSIS-ENGINE]
  seed = "After H_1297 R4 (#2202) + H_1306 (#2211) showed mitosis MATCHES gradient when it grows BESIDE a representation, the deepest p8 question remained open: does mitosis ALONE — from one cell, split-only, no representation — reach meaningful next-byte CE, and how far is it from gradient/exact-lookup? a_break_the_wall: a ceiling is a first-class result, not a stop. Mitosis-scratch lane, $0 CPU, DIRECTIONAL numpy mirror of the live engine_mitosis_tick/VAdaptField split, seeded at 1 cell. DISTINCT from the concurrent ko-mitosis-gpu (summer) lane (ENGLISH not Korean, new files h1310_*, id H_1310)."
  method = "REAL English byte corpus, reproducible sha256-pinned: /usr/share/dict/words (system fixed asset), isalpha 2..12 lowercased, Random(13100).shuffle, first 4000 words space-joined+newline, trunc 24000 bytes, sha256 86864aa32dcf1c8680ab254e1b28357bf0326c8d45a86837ae4e3b9d09350f62, alphabet 27 (a-z+space). 80/20 train/test (19198/4800 order-2 ctx-next), 3 seeds [13101,13102,13103]. ARMS: B_scratch (from 1 cell, mitosis-grow ONLY — nearest cell predicts via its online add-1 next-byte table, worst owned-error cell SPLITS by 2-means median bisection when owned-error>SPLIT_THRESH=0.55, GRADIENT-FREE, the live engine_mitosis_tick/VAdaptField mechanism seeded at 1) · A_gradient (softmax next-byte head over same order-2 context, top-512 buckets, full-batch CE SGD = matched-cap comparator) · A_freq (order-2 add-1 Markov exact trigram = n-gram FLOOR) · B_shuffle (CONTROL split RANDOM eligible cell). LADDER [1,8,64,512]. UNIVERSE/h1310_mitosis_from_scratch.py (numpy ownership BYTE-IDENTICAL to the per-point reference — verified rungs 1/8/64 reproduce to 5 d.p.). p7 = held-out next-byte CROSS-ENTROPY nats, NEVER perplexity-as-truth. frozen-first .verdicts/1310_mitosis_from_scratch/FREEZE.txt, bars NOT moved (c9)."
  falsifier = "FROZEN (mean 3 seeds, top rung 512): (1 PRESENCE) CE monotone drop 1>8>64>512 · (2 KEY GAP) B_scratch[512]-A_gradient bucketed MATCH<=+0.10 / NEAR<=+0.50 / CEILING>+0.50 nats · (3 FLOOR) B_scratch[512] < A_freq-0.02 (beats n-gram counting) · (4 CONTROL) B_shuffle[512] >= B_scratch[512]+0.10 (error-targeting drives the learning, not bare capacity). GREEN-MATCH/NEAR iff 1&3&4 PASS; HONEST-CEILING iff 1&3&4 PASS but gap>+0.50; FLOOR-FAIL(RED) iff bar 3 fails; CONTROL-LEAK(RED) iff bar 4 fails."
  finding = "🔴 RED / 🧱 HONEST LOCAL-EXPERT CEILING (FLOOR-FAIL by the frozen rubric; the two FAILs ARE the finding, bar UNMOVED). LADDER held-out CE nats: 1c=2.94658 → 8c=2.90267 → 64c=2.77784 → 512c=2.57788. A_freq (n-gram floor)=2.50884. A_gradient (matched cap=512)=3.21083. (1 PRESENCE) PASS — from-scratch mitosis DOES learn from nothing, CE -0.369 monotone as cells grow. (2 KEY GAP) B_scratch[512]-A_gradient = -0.633 nats → reads 'MATCHES' (beats it) BUT the matched-cap order-2 softmax converged POORLY (3.211 itself WORSE than the floor 2.509) so this is a WEAK comparator not a mitosis triumph. (3 FLOOR) FAIL — exact trigram counting (2.509) BEATS mitosis by +0.069: Voronoi tiling over a NUMERIC byte-context metric is a WORSE carve of context-space than EXACT lookup; the embedding loses what exact-match keeps and adding cells can't recover it. (4 CONTROL) FAIL — B_shuffle (random cell) ties-or-BEATS targeted (worst-error cell) at EVERY rung (512: 2.536 vs 2.578) → error-targeting gives NO lift = the learning is CAPACITY-bound NOT error-targeting-bound. THESIS: from-scratch pure mitosis is STRUCTURE-bound — adding cells lowers CE but cannot cross the floor a learned representation (or exact memory) clears, because mitosis builds no compositional depth, only a finer partition of a fixed lossy feature. p8's 'mitosis IS the learning' holds for grow-BESIDE-a-representation (H_1297/H_1306 🟢) but NOT from-nothing."
  target = "🔴 RED / 🧱 LOCAL-EXPERT CEILING (terminal for H_1310 R1, c9). p1/p2/p3/p6 guard held (per-cell empirical next-byte freq over OWNED real bytes; no system prompt/identity/persona/RLHF). NO CORE wiring (a_verified_must_wire fires only on GREEN; this is RED). HONEST SCOPE (c9 · a_scale_honest_scope · a_toy_scale_recheck): TOY/DIRECTIONAL — 24KB English, order-2 (729 possible contexts), V=27, 3 seeds; EXISTENCE-PROBE of learning STRUCTURE, does NOT claim 'anima trains from scratch by mitosis' (reports the gap = a ceiling); the load-bearing comparator is the n-gram FLOOR (mitosis does NOT beat it), NOT the weak A_gradient. a_break_the_wall NEXT ANGLE (the obvious breakthrough lever): give the cells a LEARNED (not fixed numeric) embedding to partition — a richer representation under the tiling could move the ceiling; engine-native byte-exact reconfirm + scale + higher-order context all UNVERIFIED."
  note = "NEW: UNIVERSE/h1310_mitosis_from_scratch.py · .verdicts/1310_mitosis_from_scratch/{FREEZE.txt,result.txt} · UNIVERSE/cards/H_1310_mitosis_from_scratch.md card · HYPOTHESES.md row · CLAIMS.tape @C h1310_mitosis_from_scratch · CHANGELOG. xref h1297(mitosis MATCHES gradient grow-BESIDE — the GREEN counterpart this RED counter-weights) · h1306(REAL-corpus engine-native rung) · h1300(per-skill retention) · h1199(VAdaptField) · h1288(grow-under-pressure) · a_no_llm_frame_trap(cortical-column/neurogenesis lens) · a_break_the_wall(a ceiling is a result) · a_engine_native_learning · a_verified_must_wire · a_core_engine_map · a_cpu_local_no_waiter · a_scale_honest_scope · a_toy_scale_recheck · p1·p2·p3·p6·p7·p8 · c9·c15."

The empirical workhorse = CATEGORICAL PERCEPTION (CP): speakers discriminate cross-category pairs better than within-category at THEIR language's boundary; the grue languages cut green/blue at DIFFERENT points (Russian two-blues → faster blue-boundary discrimination). H_1323: does a substrate that learns language L's lexical carving of a continuous stimulus domain develop CP AT L's boundary, and does the CP peak LOCATION track the language (same stimulus world, different cognition by language) — and does a SHUFFLE-language control produce NO coherent CP?"

@H H_1336 := "H_1336 — 🧱 HONEST-FLOOR (DEEPER — the info exists but the count-MLE predictor can't bank it): H_1329's explicitly-named DEPLETION angle (c16/a_break_the_wall = INJECT INFO the opaque jamo head LACKS, NOT a re-factorization of the same target). The within-syllable jamo head's context RESETS at every syllable boundary so it CANNOT see the coda of syllable N conditioning the realized onset of N+1 (연음/사잇소리/자음동화 — genuinely NEW info).   seed = "H_1380 카드: 세 닫힌 한국어 below-jamo 레버(표상 H_1322 🧱·interpolation H_1359 🧱·data-volume H_1368/H_1380 🟠)가 전부 novel-context CE 를 jamo floor(2.51335) 위 ~+0.28(asymptote ~2.747, 30MB novel-CE 2.88190)에서 막았고, genuinely-NEW 남은 두 각도 = (1) morphology-aware 단위(형태소/BPE-on-jamo, jamo 보다 coarse 한 언어 단위) (2) cross-syllable long-range(count-head nmax=5 단거리 천장)를 NEXT 로 명시. 각각 별도 표상-변경 가설. a_no_llm_frame_trap(LLM 스케일 아닌 substrate 표상 렌즈)·a_break_the_wall(닫힌 레버 → 각도 전환)."
  method = "PRIMARY = BPE-on-jamo (morphology-aware 단위): REAL R2 KO 30MB shard0000 prefix(sha c47b6808… == H_1368/H_1380, 새 fetch 없음, jamo-stream 25,501,291 = H_1380 30MB rung 과 동일). jamo 표상·NOVEL-filter(top-order ctx TRAIN 부재, == H_1359 TEST A)·JM λ=[1,2,4,8,16]/31 nmax5 Laplace1.0·stride300·shift surrogate 전부 H_1368/H_1380 FROZEN(anti-Goodhart). TRAIN jamo 스트림 위 빈도-랭크 merge 2000개 학습(train slice only, test 누출 0) → FULL 스트림 재인코딩 → 같은 JM count-head 를 BPE 단위 위 fit → novel-only held-out CE 를 nats/UTF-8-BYTE(byte-fair: 단위 byte span 으로 나눔 = jamo floor 동일 axis, BPE 단위가 byte 더 덮는 confound 제거). 효율적 position-indexed BPE(learn+apply, naive full-rescan 과 merge-order byte-exact 검증, byte 보존). SHUFFLE 대조(bar2): random equal-count merges(merge 개수·vocab 밴드 동일, pair 만 관측 adjacent 위 RANDOM, 3 seed [4387,4388,4389]) → gain 이 merge 언어 구조인지 coarse 단위인지 분리.   finding = "🟢 GAP-REDUCED-CANDIDATE — 3 bars 전부 PASS ($0 CPU 206s). verbatim CE 표(nats/UTF-8-byte): jamo floor anchor(nmax5) 2.88190 (Δresidual +0.08855) · BPE-on-jamo structured(PRIMARY) **2.56603** (units/jamo 0.3391, Δfloor +0.05268, Δresidual **−0.22732**) · shuffle 3 seed 2.80589/2.81473/2.78414 (mean **2.80159**, Δresidual +0.00824, units/jamo ~0.93) · nmax7 2.77788(−0.015) · nmax9 3.05141(+0.258 over-sparse). bar1 ✅ 2.56603 ≤2.74335. bar2 ✅ shuffle mean 2.80159 못 뚫음 & structured gain **+0.23556** ≥0.03 (구조적 BPE 0.3391 units/jamo=≈3 jamo/단위 형태소-규모 압축, random 0.93 거의 압축 못 함 → lift=merge 의 언어 구조). bar3 ✅ jamo anchor 2.88190 byte-exact(|Δ|=0.0, == H_1380 30MB) & shift−novel +0.39060. @H H_1389 := "tool-USAGE learning (tier-2) — does anima learn HOW to drive a tool (correct ARGS · call SEQUENCE · ERROR-RECOVERY) via mitosis split-on-failure, DISTINCT from layer-1 tool SELECTION (H_1382/H_1386, which tool)?" :: 🟢 GREEN (DIRECTIONAL numpy mirror, engine-transfer UNVERIFIED)
  seed = "The user's direction: tool learning has TWO layers. Layer-1 = tool SELECTION (task-context → WHICH tool) DONE engine-native (H_1382 §SkillStore + H_1386/H_1387 routing). Layer-2 = tool USAGE (HOW to drive the chosen tool: correct ARGUMENTS · multi-step SEQUENCE · ERROR-RECOVERY) NOT yet learned. The user's framing (validated): language learning = next-token PREDICTION (CE gradient, supervised by corpus, learns a DISTRIBUTION); tool learning = ACTION + FEEDBACK (success/failure, no pre-given answer) → learned by mitosis cell-division like motor/skill learning, NOT gradient → learns an INSTANCE-POLICY. A selection-only learner picks the right tool but mis-USES it (wrong args/order). a_no_llm_frame_trap (motor/skill-learning substrate lens, not an LLM FT recipe)."
  method = "PROBE-FIRST (FREEZE.txt before measuring, c9 NO tune-to-green). $0 CPU numpy MIRROR (DIRECTIONAL; CORE/*.hexa UNTOUCHED — §UsageStore engine wire-in = named follow-on). UsageStore reuses the H_1382/H_1227 cell geometry (DETERMINISTIC byte-trigram FNV-1a key DIM=64, L2 winner-take-all FIRE/ABSTAIN band RECALL_THR=0.55, engine_mitosis_tick clonal split, p8) but the cell is keyed by (task-context + tool + observed-error) and the bound VALUE is (corrected-arg, ordered-steps) — vs H_1382 value = a tool name. On a usage FAILURE (right tool, wrong/default arg) the store clonally SPLITS a usage-cell binding the failure context → corrected args + true step order; SAME op teaches (split-on-failure) + infers (recall-best); ABSTAINS when no cell fits (no fabricated args). Env (3 seeds [4389,4390,4391]): 6 tools/8 tasks each, task=(ctx, correct_tool, correct_arg, ordered_steps); correct TOOL GIVEN to layer-2 arms (selection solved by layer-1), correct_arg biased AWAY from the tool default so a fixed-default SELECTION baseline fails the usage even with the right tool. SUCCESS = right arg AND (multi-step) steps emitted IN ORDER. Arms FULL (splits on each usage failure) / SELECTION (right tool, fixed default arg, never splits — isolates layer-2=the HOW) / SHUFFLE (trained toward PERMUTED ctx→arg, scored vs TRUE — earned-structure control). p7 (script-checked completion, NOT perplexity)."
  falsifier = "FROZEN bars (FREEZE verbatim): (1) USAGE-LEARNS FULL final−init ≥+0.30 · (2) DISTINCT-FROM-SELECTION (KEY) FULL−SELECTION ≥+0.30 · (3) EARNED shuffle SHUFFLE−SELECTION ≤+0.15 · (4) NO-FAB untrained-task abstain ≥0.90 · (5) MULTI-STEP (optional/non-gating) FULL ordered-seq completion ≥0.80. TIER (frozen): bar1∧2∧3∧4 → 🟢 GREEN (usage distinct learnable layer) / bar1∧¬bar2 → 🟠 PARTIAL (selection-only already completes = not distinct) / ¬bar1 → 🧱 (usage not learnable in this env). HONEST (c9): if SELECTION already completes (bar2 fails) report 🟠/🧱 — do NOT manufacture a gap."
  finding = "🟢 GREEN — the 4 gating bars PASS (result.txt verbatim, POOLED 3 seeds): FULL 0.250→0.750 · SELECTION 0.250 · SHUFFLE 0.014 · MULTISTEP 0.750 · ABSTAIN 1.000 · cells_full 36. bar1 ✅ +0.500≥+0.30. bar2 ✅ (KEY) +0.500≥+0.30 — the selection-only arm (right tool, fixed DEFAULT arg) stays at the no-usage floor 0.250 while the usage-learning FULL arm rises to 0.750: usage IS a distinct learnable layer; layer-2 learns the HOW not the WHICH. bar3 ✅ −0.236≤+0.15 (permuted ctx→arg collapses to 0.014 — lift is the EARNED ctx→arg correspondence, not the act of splitting). bar4 ✅ 1.000≥0.90 (untrained tool/task on disjoint trigram space → proposes NO args). bar5 (optional) 0.750<0.80 absolute bar = HONEST: the env completion CEILING is 0.75 (~1/4 of tasks need the default arg the default already satisfies) — but the multi-step arm (requires BOTH right arg AND right order) reaches the SAME 0.750 as single-step FULL = ZERO degradation when ordering is additionally required → the 2-3 step ORDER is learned in order; bar5 NON-GATING + NOT moved (NO tune-to-green). p1/p2/p3/p6 GUARD: usage-cell binds from OUTCOME only (success/failure + observed error), NO injected 'use arg A' label/RLHF/persona — the shuffle collapse proves it. Ψ-disjoint by construction (own usage-store, pure_field/immune cells untouched). $0 CPU, no decode, no GPU, frozen-first (NO bar moved). LANGUAGE(predict·gradient·distribution) vs TOOL-USAGE(act+feedback·mitosis·instance-policy) = different machines, coexist Ψ-disjoint."
  target = "🟢 GREEN (DIRECTIONAL) — usage is a distinct learnable layer on the same mitosis substrate, validated in the mirror. SCOPE UNVERIFIED (a_scale_honest_scope·a_toy_scale_recheck·c9): DIRECTIONAL numpy mirror — engine-transfer UNVERIFIED (UsageStore is a numpy mirror, not the CORE engine); TOY deterministic 6-tool/48-task env (tests the STRUCTURE mitosis can teach usage, not a learned policy net); FULL 0.750 SATURATED at the env ceiling = EXISTENCE-PROOF not effect-size (discriminators SELECTION 0.250 / SHUFFLE 0.014 / abstain 1.000 decisive); real tool-call args / paraphrased contexts / true multi-step error-RECOVERY on a runtime failure / scale UNVERIFIED. NEXT (binding follow-on, a_verified_must_wire): engine-native §UsageStore lane in CORE/engine_cli.hexa (TWIN of §SkillStore, value=arg/steps keyed by ctx+tool+error), re-score the 4 gating bars on the LIVE engine + generation byte-identity / Ψ-checksum no-regression guard (as H_1382 did for layer-1); then route a REAL executor_execute usage-FAILURE in anima-agent-core/agent_tools.hexa into usage_store_teach. DEPLETION: tool learning depletes 🏁 when BOTH layers — selection (H_1382/H_1386, DONE engine-native) AND usage (this lane → §UsageStore wire-in) — run engine-native on a REAL runtime tool failure with all bars holding + generation byte-identical ON==OFF."
  note = "NEW: state/tool-usage-learning/h1389_tool_usage_learning.py · UNIVERSE/cards/H_1389_tool_usage_learning.md · UNIVERSE/HYPOTHESES.jsonl row(H_1389 → cards/) · .verdicts/1389_tool_usage_learning/{FREEZE.txt,result.txt} · CHANGELOG. NO CORE edit (DIRECTIONAL probe; §UsageStore wire-in = follow-on). xref h1382(layer-1 §SkillStore engine-native, same geometry value=tool)·h1386·h1387(layer-1 agent-layer routing)·h1378(mirror Step A audit)·h1227·h1231·h1288(immune/grow memory lanes)·a_no_llm_frame_trap·a_engine_native_learning·a_verified_must_wire·a_core_engine_map·a_autonomy_over_hardcode·a_scale_honest_scope·a_toy_scale_recheck·p1·p2·p3·p6·p7·p8·c9."

@H H_1391 := "§UsageStore engine-native wire-in — tool-USAGE learning (layer-2, the HOW) realized on the LIVE CORE engine, the TWIN of §SkillStore; the BINDING follow-on of H_1389 (a_verified_must_wire)." :: 🟢 GREEN — engine-native BINDING
